  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Casimir III, leader of Poland (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Poland
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Winged Hussar, Replacing Lancer (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Pancerny, Replacing Heavy Skirmisher (Requires technology: Physics)
        - Unique Building: Ducal Stable, Replacing Stable (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Building: Barbican, Replacing Armory (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Civilization Ability: Gain 1 Free Social Policy when you enter the Classical, Renaissance, Modern and Information Eras. Gain 2 additional Free Tenets when you adopt an Ideology for the first time.
      - Leader: Casimir III
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      
      ## Policies
      - Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Transnationalism: Each turn, increases the chance that a Corporate Franchise will appear in a foreign City to 15% (normally 5%). Global Franchise maximum increased by 25%, and every owned Town you have built counts as a Franchise. +2 Culture from all Corporation Offices.
      - Universal Healthcare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Healthcare: Receive a free Hospital in every City. +50 when a Citizen is born in any City, scaling with Era.
      - Their Finest Hour (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Their Finest Hour: All cities get +2 Air Unit Slots. Each Air Unit stationed in a city increases the City's Defense by 3. Can build B17 Bombers.
      - Arsenal of Democracy (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Arsenal of Democracy: +15% Production towards Military Units. +10 Influence with all known City-States when you expend Great People, and +40 Influence when you gift Units to City-States.
      - Avant Garde (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Avant Garde: The rate at which Great People are born is increased by 33%. -2 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Self Determination (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Self Determination: Liberating a city (including removing another civ's Sphere of Influence on a City-State through conquest) gives 15 XP to all units, 50 Influence with all City-States, and 40 Science, scaling with Era and City Population. The liberated city gains an Arsenal and 6 units.
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Players lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 5

        - DeceptiveBias: 4
        - Rationale: Tweaked by In-Game AI (Turn 364: Adjusting persona for wartime. Russia's declaration forces us from peaceful science mode to defensive war posture. Boldness:5->8 to resist Russian aggression, WarBias:1->6 to enable counter-offensives, HostileBias:3->8 to treat Russia as mortal threat, FriendlyBias:10->3 because ideologically aligned 'allies' like Inca betrayed us with Cold War proposal. DiplomaticBalance:10->5 - no more appeasement, survival requires decisive action.)
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Computers
        - Rationale: Turn 372: After completing Nuclear Fission (5 turns remaining), Computers is essential. It grants +1 Trade Route and unlocks Carrier for air power projection. More importantly, Computers leads to Telecommunications → Satellites → Nanotechnology → Future Tech - the critical path for science victory. Combined Arms (Tanks) would help against Russia but we have nuclear deterrent now; closing the tech gap with Korea (67 vs 65) is paramount.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Transnationalism (Policy)
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Maintaining Transnationalism as next Freedom tenet. With trade routes disrupted by war and Babylon-Shoshone peace changing dynamics, the corporate franchise mechanics (15% appearance rate, +25% global max, +2 Culture from Offices) provide economic resilience. After this, consider Arsenal of Democracy for military production if Russia war escalates further.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Rome
        - Public: 5
        - Private: 5
        - Rationale: Turn 321: Normalizing Rome relationship from -5/-5 to +5/+5. Rome is now a vassal of Inca (Freedom-aligned), creating bloc solidarity. They maintain DoF with us and are distant non-threats. The previous negative modifiers reflected their vassal obligations during Inca-Russia tensions, but with Inca adopting Freedom and ideological alignment achieved, positive relations with Rome support coalition cohesion. Sanctions against Rome are a World Congress matter; bilateral relations should remain constructive.
        - UpdatedTurn: 321
        
        ### Polynesia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Turn 356: Resetting Polynesia relationship from +10/+10 to 0/0. Critical development: Polynesia has capitulated to Shoshone and is now their vassal (Peace Treaty, Master relationship visible). Polynesia is currently at war with our allies Babylon, Korea, and Inca as part of Shoshone's Order bloc. Maintaining positive relations with a vassal of our main enemy is diplomatically inconsistent and could confuse AI threat assessment. Neutral stance acknowledges their compromised position while avoiding premature war declaration.
        - UpdatedTurn: 357
        
        ### Segou
        - Public: 10
        - Private: 10
        - Rationale: Turn 360: Increasing relationship with Segou from neutral to +10/+10. Segou just became our ally (switched from Korea) and they are strategically located near Shoshone territory. With Shoshone's Decolonization proposal threatening all city-state influence, having strong positive relations with Segou ensures they remain in our sphere. They're also the proposer of Babylon's Sphere of Influence proposal, so good relations help our coalition coordination.
        - UpdatedTurn: 360
        
        ### The Inca
        - Public: -15
        - Private: -20
        - Rationale: Turn 368: Slightly improving Inca relationship from -20/-25 to -15/-20. While they remain suspect for their Cold War proposal that threatened our trade income, they are Freedom-aligned and voting against Decolonization. Cannot afford to alienate all allies. Their 10 delegates matter for World Congress. Keeping negative modifiers acknowledges their betrayal but allows functional alliance against greater threats (Russia, Shoshone).
        - UpdatedTurn: 368
        
        ### Korea
        - Public: 10
        - Private: 5
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Korea has overtaken us in tech (67 vs 65) making them our primary Science Victory competitor. However, they remain critical Freedom ally with 10 World Congress votes for our World Ideology: Freedom proposal in 9 turns. Raising from +5/0 to +10/+5 - we need their votes and they remain ideologically aligned, but we must acknowledge the tech competition.
        - UpdatedTurn: 372
        
        ### Babylon
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 45
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Babylon remains our staunchest Freedom ally with 16 World Congress delegates. They just made peace with Shoshone - this reduces immediate military pressure but we need to ensure they still support our World Ideology: Freedom proposal. Their cultural victory progress (5 influential civs) is secondary concern. Strengthening bond to +40/+45 for World Congress coordination.
        - UpdatedTurn: 372
        
        ### Russia
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Russia continues aggressive war despite our nuclear capability. They have superior military (6111 vs 4591) and are pushing our borders. Their Autocracy ideology vs our Freedom makes them existential threat. Maintaining maximum hostility - we have nuclear weapons now and must use them if they push further into our territory. War score is only -4% but they killed our units near Warsaw.
        - UpdatedTurn: 372
        
        ### The Shoshone
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -40
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Shoshone made peace with Babylon (critical development). Their massive military (11392) is still a threat but immediate pressure reduced. Order ideology remains opposed to our Freedom. Public neutral to avoid provoking war while we handle Russia, private -40 acknowledges their long-term threat and Decolonization proposal threat to our city-state alliances.
        - UpdatedTurn: 372
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Turn 372: Adjusting for critical science sprint while managing Russian aggression. Korea overtook us in tech (67 vs 65) - maximum Science priority. Increased Defense to 85 as Russia pushing borders aggressively. UseNuke to 90 (from 80) - now have actual nuclear weapons for deterrence. Increased Espionage to 95 to steal tech from Shoshone/Korea to close gap. Reduced Offense to 35 - defensive war only. Maintained high Diplomacy (85) for World Congress Freedom vote in 9 turns. Babylon-Shoshone peace reduces immediate threat but we must prepare for long-term science race.
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 45
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 8 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Polish Sphere of Influence: La Venta:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Sanction: The Shoshone:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: 326
          - Endowment for the Arts:
            - Description: Increases up to 33% generation of Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians. Decreases up to 33% generation of Great Scientists, Engineers, and Merchants.
              Civilizations with Scores from Policies and Great Works below the global median will receive a boost to Production and Culture up to 33%.
              Details: The boosts are proportional to the difference between the score of the Civilization and the leader.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: 359
          - World Religion: Islam:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Korean Sphere of Influence: Harappa:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 348
          - Sanction: Rome:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Shoshone Sphere of Influence: Cahokia:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 348
          - Open Door: Raqmu:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Babylonian Sphere of Influence: Djenne:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.

        You, Casimir III (leader of Poland, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 373.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Casimir III, leader of Poland (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Poland
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Winged Hussar, Replacing Lancer (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Pancerny, Replacing Heavy Skirmisher (Requires technology: Physics)
        - Unique Building: Ducal Stable, Replacing Stable (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Building: Barbican, Replacing Armory (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Civilization Ability: Gain 1 Free Social Policy when you enter the Classical, Renaissance, Modern and Information Eras. Gain 2 additional Free Tenets when you adopt an Ideology for the first time.
      - Leader: Casimir III
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      
      ## Policies
      - Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Transnationalism: Each turn, increases the chance that a Corporate Franchise will appear in a foreign City to 15% (normally 5%). Global Franchise maximum increased by 25%, and every owned Town you have built counts as a Franchise. +2 Culture from all Corporation Offices.
      - Universal Healthcare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Healthcare: Receive a free Hospital in every City. +50 when a Citizen is born in any City, scaling with Era.
      - Their Finest Hour (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Their Finest Hour: All cities get +2 Air Unit Slots. Each Air Unit stationed in a city increases the City's Defense by 3. Can build B17 Bombers.
      - Arsenal of Democracy (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Arsenal of Democracy: +15% Production towards Military Units. +10 Influence with all known City-States when you expend Great People, and +40 Influence when you gift Units to City-States.
      - Avant Garde (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Avant Garde: The rate at which Great People are born is increased by 33%. -2 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Self Determination (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Self Determination: Liberating a city (including removing another civ's Sphere of Influence on a City-State through conquest) gives 15 XP to all units, 50 Influence with all City-States, and 40 Science, scaling with Era and City Population. The liberated city gains an Arsenal and 6 units.
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Players lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Computers
        - Rationale: Unlocks Carrier for naval air power projection, grants +1 Trade Route for economic strength, and is the direct gateway to Telecommunications -> Satellites for our spaceship victory sprint.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Policy: Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom)
        - Rationale: Our corporation, Centaurus Extractors, is highly active. Transnationalism boosts franchise spread and gives culture from offices and towns, which provides critical economic resilience and gold to fund spaceship parts later.
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 10
        - Private: 10
        - Rationale: The Inca are a key Freedom ally with 9 World Congress delegates. With our World Ideology: Freedom proposal voting in 8 turns, we must solidify relations with them to secure their votes and guarantee our ideological dominance.
        - TargetID: 6
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Korea is our strongest partner and fellow Freedom believer. Slightly raising relations to ensure lock-step coordination on the World Ideology proposal and mutual defense against the Autocracy/Order blocs.
        - Private: 10
        - TargetID: 0
        - Public: 15
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-persona
      - args:
        - FriendlyBias: 6
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 9
        - WorkWithWillingness: 9
        - Rationale: Adjusting persona to maximize cooperation and trust within the Freedom coalition. Raising Friendliness, FriendlyBias, and WorkWith/Against willingness to ensure our allies (Babylon, Korea, Inca) remain tightly bound to us for the upcoming World Congress votes and our joint defense against Russia.
        - Friendliness: 8
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
        - Flavors:
          - Offense: 50
          - Defense: 80
          - Gold: 75
          - Naval: 70
          - Air: 80
      - rationale: Transitioning to a more active counter-offensive posture against Russia now that we have battleships and a local military advantage. Increasing Offense to 50 to allow the AI to push for St. Petersburg. Boosting Naval and Air flavors to leverage our technical upgrades. Maintaining Spaceship grand strategy and maximum Science/Espionage to sprint for the tech lead.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 70646
    - reasoningTokens: 10557
    - outputTokens: 441
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Researching New Technologies**
            
            My focus is now on optimizing our technological advancement. Having just unlocked Nuclear Fission, I'm evaluating the best path forward, with Computers as the leading contender, to ensure we maintain our strategic edge.
            
            **Selecting Next Research**
            
            Having just concluded Nuclear Fission, my attention turns to charting our subsequent technological trajectory. My current thoughts are occupied with the decision between 'Computers' and 'Combined Arms' as the next research endeavor, ensuring we continue to advance our capabilities effectively.
            
            **Deciding on Research Path**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our research direction, confirming Nuclear Fission is complete. My current focus is on whether 'Computers' is already underway, and if not, if it's the optimal next step versus 'Combined Arms', especially considering our ongoing conflict with Russia and our Science victory aspirations. I'm also reviewing available Ideology Policies.
            
            **Assessing Immediate Threats**
            
            Given our ongoing conflicts with Russia and Lhasa, and noting our current military disadvantage and war weariness, I am prioritizing research that will directly address these threats. Military advancements and unit support are now at the forefront of my strategic considerations, overriding broader long-term research plans for now.
            
            **Evaluating Current Engagements**
            
            I'm assessing our tactical positions, noting significant friendly strengths in Krakow and St. Petersburg, but a concerning deficit in Moscow. Our new battleships and landships are proving effective, and I'm reviewing upcoming World Congress votes, particularly the Freedom ideology proposal.
            
            **Securing World Ideology**
            
            I'm focused on ensuring our proposed World Ideology: Freedom passes in the World Congress. We have a strong delegate count, so my immediate priority is confirming our allies' support and solidifying those relationships to guarantee a favorable outcome, especially given the potential pressure it will place on Russia and Shoshone.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Support**
            
            I'm currently strategizing to secure crucial votes for our World Ideology proposal. My focus is on assessing and potentially improving relationships with key city-states, like the Inca, to guarantee their support in the upcoming World Congress, especially considering our competition with the Shoshone.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Ties**
            
            I'm prioritizing our diplomatic relationships to ensure the World Ideology: Freedom proposal passes. My current focus is on strategically adjusting our standing with the Inca, increasing our alliance's strength for their crucial votes.
            
            **Reviewing Technological Advancements**
            
            My thoughts are shifting back to our technological progress. With Nuclear Fission complete and resources like aluminum and uranium available, I'm considering the implications and next steps for our scientific development.
            
            **Checking Resource Availability**
            
            My attention has shifted to verifying our Uranium reserves and those of other nations, as it's not currently listed as an available resource for us, despite recent technological advancements. I'm cross-referencing player data to understand our strategic position.
            
            **Verifying Resource Acquisition**
            
            I am currently verifying our Uranium reserves and cross-referencing player data, as Uranium isn't listed as an available resource despite researching Nuclear Fission. I'm also exploring trade options for Uranium with allied nations like Korea, Babylon, and the Inca.
            
            **Securing Critical Resources**
            
            I'm now focused on the immediate need for oil, as our Destroyer production will exacerbate a deficit, leading to unit combat penalties. I'm assessing our current trade options with allies like Korea and Babylon, while also investigating potential sources such as Russia and the Shoshone.
            
            **Acquiring Essential Resources**
            
            I'm now urgently focused on securing oil due to impending Destroyer production needs. Russia holds significant oil reserves, including the recently occupied Quebec City, which explains their abundance. I'm re-evaluating trade possibilities with allies and considering other sources, while simultaneously checking if Computer research alleviates fuel requirements for Carriers.
            
            **Prioritizing Science Victory**
            
            My focus is now on accelerating our Science Victory. With Computers researched, unlocking crucial advancements like Telecommunications and Satellites, I'm confirming this aligns with our Grand Strategy and our competitive standing with Korea and the Inca, ensuring a sprint to victory.
            
            **Adopting Freedom Tenets**
            
            I'm prioritizing which Freedom tenets to adopt next. With Space Procurements already secured, I'm evaluating Transnationalism's benefits, especially since we have the Centaurus Extractors corporation. I'm also reviewing Universal Healthcare as another strong contender for our next policy.
            
            **Selecting Next Freedom Tenet**
            
            I'm deciding on our next Freedom tenet. Avant Garde, with its boost to Great People generation, is particularly appealing. I'm weighing this against the strategic advantages of other options like Universal Healthcare, ensuring our choices maximize our progress towards victory.
            
            **Selecting Next Freedom Tenet**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our next Freedom tenet. While Avant Garde seems powerful for Great People, our Scientist generation is already strong. Transnationalism supports our corporation, crucial for spaceship parts. However, Universal Healthcare's food and recovery boosts for population growth warrant careful consideration against these.
            
            **Reviewing Ideology Adoption**
            
            I'm reviewing our current ideology tenet progress, noting we've maxed out Rationalism and have 8 Freedom tenets. I'm weighing whether to finish the Progress branch or adopt another Freedom tenet like Transnationalism, which supports our corporation. My gold reserves are surprisingly low, prompting a closer look at expenses.
            
            **Purchasing Defensive Units**
            
            I'm reviewing our finances and realizing our gold reserves are low because the AI wisely purchased Winged Hussars to defend against Russia. Despite paying significant gold to the Inca, our trade routes are generating substantial income, ensuring we can sustain operations and continue our victory pursuit.
            
            **Bolstering Defensive Strength**
            
            I'm reassessing our current military engagements. While the AI's purchase of Winged Hussars to counter Russia was prudent, my analysis of our front lines in Krakow, Wroclaw, and St. Petersburg reveals a significant strength advantage. Furthermore, our newly upgraded Battleships offer potent coastal bombardment capabilities, allowing us to project power directly onto enemy cities.
            
            **Strengthening Alliances**
            
            I'm reassessing our diplomatic stances. While Lhasa is a minor concern allied with Russia, my focus is on bolstering key relationships. I'm increasing our standing with Korea to ensure their full support and maintaining our strong partnership with Babylon, crucial given their war with Russia. My current assessment of our relationship with the Inca shows room for significant improvement.
            
            **Securing Critical Delegates**
            
            I'm focused on securing the Inca's nine delegates for the World Ideology: Freedom vote. I've adjusted our diplomatic stance to Public 10, Private 10, ensuring their support, as this is a critical step towards victory. I'm also noting Rome's alignment with Inca as a Freedom follower.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Nuances**
            
            I'm navigating a complex web of alliances and vassalage, particularly concerning Polynesia and their master, Shoshone. While Polynesia is forced into conflict with our allies due to their vassal status, I intend to maintain neutrality to avoid further escalation. Russia remains an enemy, and I'm preparing for offensive action.
            
            **Stealing Crucial Technologies**
            
            My current focus is on accelerating our technological advancement by leveraging our extensive spy network within Shoshone. The intelligence gathered indicates we are on the verge of stealing several critical technologies, which will be instrumental in closing the gap with our rivals.
            
            **Optimizing Flavor Profile**
            
            I'm reviewing our current flavor profiles against our Spaceship Grand Strategy. With nearly all optimized, I'm now scrutinizing espionage agent actions in Sokoto and Manila for city-state influence, and considering if any minor flavor adjustments are necessary before proceeding.
            
            **Increasing Offensive Stance**
            
            I'm reassessing our military posture on the Russian front. Given our current strength advantage in key sectors like Krakow and St. Petersburg, and the recent Battleship upgrades, I believe increasing our Offense from 35 is warranted. Capturing St. Petersburg is a critical objective for crippling Russia's science and production.
            
            **Adjusting Military Offense**
            
            I'm fine-tuning our AI's offensive capabilities. Increasing Offense to 50 while maintaining a strong Defense at 80 should allow our forces to capitalize on opportunities, like capturing St. Petersburg, without becoming overly reckless, ensuring our overall strategic goals remain on track.
            
            **Analyzing Delegate Strength**
            
            My focus is on the World Ideology vote. If Freedom passes, our delegate count will significantly increase, from 9 to 14. This surge, driven by all 5 Freedom civs gaining 5 delegates each, will solidify our position and pave the way for victory.
            
            **Securing Coalition Delegates**
            
            I'm focused on ensuring our Freedom coalition secures overwhelming delegate strength. Raising relationships with Korea and the Inca is paramount for maximizing our delegate count to 75, guaranteeing control over future World Congress votes and paving the way for a Diplomatic Victory, should we choose it.
            
            **Adjusting Persona Parameters**
            
            I am reviewing our persona settings to better reflect our current diplomatic strategy. I plan to increase FriendlyBias and Friendliness, alongside WorkWithWillingness and WorkAgainstWillingness, to foster stronger alliances and effectively counter our adversaries like Russia and the Shoshone.
            
            **Selecting Next Ideology Tenet**
            
            I am re-evaluating our next ideology tenet. While Transnationalism supports our corporation, Universal Healthcare offers a significant immediate advantage by providing free hospitals, boosting growth and science across all our cities, a crucial advantage for our victory pursuit.
            
            **Confirming Resource Redundancy**
            
            Universal Healthcare's benefit of free Hospitals is redundant as all my cities already possess Medical Labs, implying the presence of Hospitals. This means Transnationalism, which supports my corporation, becomes a more strategically sound choice for the next tenet.
            
            **Choosing Next Tenet**
            
            My current focus is on selecting the most beneficial Freedom tenet. Given the redundancy of free Hospitals and the immense economic advantage Transnationalism offers through our Centaurus Extractors corporation and numerous towns counting as franchises, it is clearly the superior choice for maximizing gold and production.
            
            **Securing Technological Advantage**
            
            I am prioritizing the acquisition of "Computers" due to its immediate benefits in unlocking Carriers, increasing trade routes, and serving as a vital precursor to crucial spaceship technologies like Telecommunications and Satellites, essential for our victory sprint. I've also adjusted relations with the Inca and Korea to solidify their support for our World Ideology proposal.
            
            **Refining Persona and Flavors**
            
            I am now adjusting our persona parameters to foster stronger alliances, increasing Friendliness and FriendlyBias to 8 and 6 respectively, and WorkWith/AgainstWillingness to 9. This aims to solidify our coalition for the World Congress and defense. I'm also updating our flavors to reflect this, increasing Offense to 50, Defense to 80, Gold to 75, and Naval to 70, to seize opportunities against Russia.
            
            **Refining Persona and Flavors**
            
            I am now adjusting our persona parameters to foster stronger alliances, increasing Friendliness and FriendlyBias to 8 and 6 respectively, and WorkWith/AgainstWillingness to 9. This aims to solidify our coalition for the World Congress and defense. I'm also updating our flavors to reflect this, increasing Offense to 50, Defense to 80, Gold to 75, and Naval to 70, to seize opportunities against Russia.
            
            **Confirming Action Parameters**
            
            I'm meticulously verifying the exact terminology for our upcoming actions. Specifically, I'm double-checking the precise names for the "Computers" technology and the "Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom)" policy to ensure flawless execution and alignment with our strategic objectives before proceeding with the planned calls.
